// Intro -> enter site

document.getElementById('enterBtn').addEventListener('click', function(){

document.getElementById('intro').classList.add('hide');

document.body.classList.remove('locked');

document.getElementById('nav').classList.add('show');

});

// Nav show on scroll (once entered) + mobile menu

window.addEventListener('scroll', function(){

if(!document.body.classList.contains('locked')){

document.getElementById('nav').classList.toggle('show', window.scrollY > 40 || true);

}

});

const burger=document.getElementById('burger'), mobmenu=document.getElementById('mobmenu'), scrim=document.getElementById('scrim');

function closeMenu(){ mobmenu.classList.remove('open'); scrim.classList.remove('open'); }

burger.addEventListener('click', function(){ mobmenu.classList.toggle('open'); scrim.classList.toggle('open'); });

scrim.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);

mobmenu.querySelectorAll('a').forEach(a=>a.addEventListener('click', closeMenu));

// Scroll reveal

const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries)=>{ entries.forEach(e=>{ if(e.isIntersecting){ e.target.classList.add('visible'); } }); }, {threshold:.15});

document.querySelectorAll('.reveal').forEach(el=>io.observe(el));

// Timeline render

const tl = document.getElementById('timeline');

CONFIG.events.forEach(ev=>{

const div=document.createElement('div');

div.className='ev reveal '+ev.cls;

div.innerHTML = `<p class="ev-name">${ev.name}</p><p class="ev-when">${ev.date}${ev.time?' · '+ev.time:''}</p><p class="ev-where">${ev.venue}</p><p class="ev-desc">${ev.desc}</p>${ev.dress?`<p class="ev-dress">DRESS: ${ev.dress}</p>`:''}`;

tl.appendChild(div); io.observe(div);

});

// Countdown

const target = new Date(CONFIG.weddingDateTimeISO).getTime();

const cdEl = document.getElementById('countdown'), cdMsg = document.getElementById('cdMsg');

function tick(){

const now = Date.now(); const diff = target - now;

if(diff <= 0){ cdEl.style.display='none'; cdMsg.style.display='block'; return; }

const d=Math.floor(diff/86400000), h=Math.floor(diff/3600000)%24, m=Math.floor(diff/60000)%60, s=Math.floor(diff/1000)%60;

cdEl.innerHTML = [[d,'DAYS'],[h,'HRS'],[m,'MIN'],[s,'SEC']].map(([v,l])=>`<div class="u"><span class="num">${v}</span><span class="lab">${l}</span></div>`).join('');

}

tick(); setInterval(tick,1000);

// Invitation envelope

const envelope=document.getElementById('envelope'), inviteCard=document.getElementById('inviteCard');

envelope.addEventListener('click', function(){

envelope.classList.toggle('open');

inviteCard.classList.toggle('show');

});

// Gallery + lightbox (scratch-to-reveal)

const gal=document.getElementById('gallery');

CONFIG.galleryImages.forEach((src,i)=>{

const item=document.createElement('div'); item.className='gitem reveal';

item.innerHTML=`<div class="ph"><img src="${src}" alt="Wedding moment ${i+1}" loading="lazy"><canvas class="scratch-canvas"></canvas></div>`;

item.addEventListener('click', ()=>openLB(i));

gal.appendChild(item); io.observe(item);

const canvas=item.querySelector('canvas');

requestAnimationFrame(()=>setupScratch(canvas));

});

function setupScratch(canvas){

const box=canvas.parentElement, w=box.clientWidth, h=box.clientHeight;

canvas.width=w; canvas.height=h;

const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');

const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,w,h);

grad.addColorStop(0,'#6E1E2B'); grad.addColorStop(1,'#A9812F');

ctx.fillStyle=grad; ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);

ctx.fillStyle='rgba(251,246,236,.85)';

ctx.font='600 '+Math.max(11,w*0.07)+'px Mukta, sans-serif';

ctx.textAlign='center'; ctx.textBaseline='middle';

ctx.fillText('✦ scratch to reveal ✦', w/2, h/2);

let drawing=false, moveCount=0;

function pos(e){

const r=canvas.getBoundingClientRect();

const p=e.touches?e.touches[0]:e;

return {x:p.clientX-r.left, y:p.clientY-r.top};

}

function reveal(x,y){

ctx.globalCompositeOperation='destination-out';

ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x,y,Math.max(28,w*0.14),0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();

}

function finish(){

canvas.classList.add('done');

setTimeout(()=>canvas.remove(), 800);

}

function start(e){ drawing=true; const p=pos(e); reveal(p.x,p.y); }

function move(e){ if(!drawing) return; e.preventDefault(); const p=pos(e); reveal(p.x,p.y); moveCount++; if(moveCount>14) finish(); }

function end(){ drawing=false; }

canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', start); canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', move); window.addEventListener('mouseup', end);

canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', start, {passive:true}); canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', move, {passive:false}); canvas.addEventListener('touchend', end);

}

const lb=document.getElementById('lightbox'), lbImg=document.getElementById('lbImg');

let lbIndex=0;

function openLB(i){ lbIndex=i; renderLB(); lb.classList.add('open'); }

function renderLB(){ lbImg.innerHTML = `<img src="${CONFIG.galleryImages[lbIndex]}" alt="">`; }

document.getElementById('lbClose').addEventListener('click', ()=>lb.classList.remove('open'));

document.getElementById('lbPrev').addEventListener('click', ()=>{ lbIndex=(lbIndex-1+CONFIG.galleryImages.length)%CONFIG.galleryImages.length; renderLB(); });

document.getElementById('lbNext').addEventListener('click', ()=>{ lbIndex=(lbIndex+1)%CONFIG.galleryImages.length; renderLB(); });

document.addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{ if(!lb.classList.contains('open'))return; if(e.key==='Escape')lb.classList.remove('open'); if(e.key==='ArrowLeft')document.getElementById('lbPrev').click(); if(e.key==='ArrowRight')document.getElementById('lbNext').click(); });

let tStartX=0;

lb.addEventListener('touchstart', e=>tStartX=e.touches[0].clientX);

lb.addEventListener('touchend', e=>{ const dx=e.changedTouches[0].clientX-tStartX; if(dx>50)document.getElementById('lbPrev').click(); if(dx<-50)document.getElementById('lbNext').click(); });

// Share button

document.getElementById('shareBtn').addEventListener('click', async ()=>{

const shareData={ title:'Ayush & Aishwarya — 21 Nov 2026', text:"You are invited to Ayush & Aishwarya's wedding!", url: location.href };

if(navigator.share){

try{ await navigator.share(shareData); return; }catch(e){ /* fall through to copy */ }

}

try{

await navigator.clipboard.writeText(location.href);

showToast('Link copied to clipboard');

}catch(e){

showToast(location.href);

}

});

function showToast(msg){ const t=document.getElementById('toast'); t.textContent=msg; t.classList.add('show'); setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'),2200); }

// Music toggle — original synthesized Indian-wedding-style piece (dhol rhythm + shehnai-like melody), no external audio file

let audioCtx=null, musicOn=false, beatTimer=null;

function dholHit(time, low){

const osc=audioCtx.createOscillator(), gain=audioCtx.createGain();

osc.type='sine'; osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(low?95:160, time);

osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(low?48:75, time+0.16);

gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.85, time);

gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, time+0.2);

osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);

osc.start(time); osc.stop(time+0.22);

}

function shehnaiNote(time, freq, dur, vol){

const osc=audioCtx.createOscillator(), lfo=audioCtx.createOscillator(), lfoGain=audioCtx.createGain(), gain=audioCtx.createGain();

osc.type='sawtooth'; osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, time);

lfo.frequency.setValueAtTime(5.5, time); lfoGain.gain.setValueAtTime(4, time);

lfo.connect(lfoGain); lfoGain.connect(osc.frequency);

gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, time);

gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(vol, time+0.06);

gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, time+dur);

osc.connect(gain); gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);

lfo.start(time); osc.start(time); lfo.stop(time+dur+0.05); osc.stop(time+dur+0.05);

}

function scheduleLoop(){

const now=audioCtx.currentTime;

// dhol taal: dha-ge-na-tin

[0,0.4,0.8,1.0,1.4,1.8].forEach((t,i)=>dholHit(now+t, i%2===0));

// a short festive melodic phrase over the beat (pentatonic, shehnai-style)

const phrase=[

[523.25,0.35],[587.33,0.35],[659.25,0.35],[783.99,0.55],

[659.25,0.35],[587.33,0.35],[523.25,0.7]

];

let t=0.1;

phrase.forEach(([f,d])=>{ shehnaiNote(now+t, f, d, 0.16); t+=d; });

beatTimer=setTimeout(scheduleLoop, 3600);

}

document.getElementById('musicBtn').addEventListener('click', function(){

if(!audioCtx){ audioCtx=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)(); }

musicOn=!musicOn;

this.classList.toggle('on', musicOn);

if(musicOn){ audioCtx.resume(); scheduleLoop(); showToast('Playing wedding music'); }

else{ clearTimeout(beatTimer); showToast('Music paused'); }

});

</script>

</body>
